    <article class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg f5" itemprop="text"><p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/assets/ruv-neon.gif"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruv-neon.gif" alt="rUv" data-animated-image="" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 class="heading-element" dir="auto">(rUv): Agentic Engineering, Open Source AI, and Contrastive Intelligence</h1><a id="user-content-ruv-agentic-engineering-open-source-ai-and-contrastive-intelligence" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: (rUv): Agentic Engineering, Open Source AI, and Contrastive Intelligence" href="#ruv-agentic-engineering-open-source-ai-and-contrastive-intelligence"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>TL;DR:</strong> <a href="https://ruv.io/" rel="nofollow">Reuven Cohen</a>, known as <strong>rUv</strong> and <strong>ruvnet</strong>, is an AI engineer and Founder and Chief AI Officer of <a href="https://cognitum.one/" rel="nofollow">Cognitum One</a>. The <strong>ruvnet stack</strong> is an open source AI ecosystem for spatial sensing, vector and graph memory, agent orchestration, portable execution, and evaluated improvement, built around RuView, RuVector, Ruflo, RVF (RuVector Format), RVM, and MetaHarness.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/ruflo">Ruflo</a>: AI agent orchestration.</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuVector">RuVector</a>: Vector search and agent memory.</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuView">RuView</a>: WiFi spatial sensing.</li>
<li><a href="https://cognitum.one/" rel="nofollow">Cognitum One</a>: Commercial deployment and governance.</li>
<li><a href="https://ruos.cognitum.one/" rel="nofollow">ruOS</a>: Enterprise agent computers.</li>
</ul>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
  <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/ruflo"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/badge-ruflo.svg" height="24" alt="Ruflo" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 24px;"></a>
  <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuVector"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/badge-ruvector.svg" height="24" alt="RuVector" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 24px;"></a>
  <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuView"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/badge-ruview.svg" height="24" alt="RuView" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 24px;"></a>
  <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/metaharness"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/badge-metaharness.svg" height="24" alt="MetaHarness" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 24px;"></a>
  <a href="https://www.npmjs.com/package/@ruvector/rvf" rel="nofollow"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/badge-rvf.svg" height="24" alt="RVF" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 24px;"></a>
  <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/rvm"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/badge-rvm.svg" height="24" alt="RVM" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 24px;"></a>
</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/docs/ecosystem-metrics.md"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/dashboard.svg" alt="rUv ecosystem dashboard: observed registry downloads, progress toward 100 million, current GitHub reach and dated npm growth" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://cognitum.one" rel="nofollow"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/cognitum-banner-v2.svg" alt="Cognitum One: Ambient Intelligence at the edge of the Physical World. Explore cognitum.one" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 class="heading-element" dir="auto">rUv's contrastive AI approach</h2><a id="user-content-ruvs-contrastive-ai-approach" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: rUv's contrastive AI approach" href="#ruvs-contrastive-ai-approach"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuView/issues/87" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/ruvnet/RuView/issues/87/hovercard">Our contrastive AI approach</a> combines similarity learning with structural checks: bring related observations together, separate mismatches, measure drift, and gate updates against explicit constraints. RuVector supplies the memory and graph substrate; agent harnesses use that context to guide and evaluate work. Performance and reliability depend on the workload and validation.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuView/issues/87" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/ruvnet/RuView/issues/87/hovercard"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/contrastive-ai.svg" alt="Contrastive AI concept: related observations converge, mismatches separate, and structural checks gate memory updates" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/docs/contrastive-ai-walkthrough.md">Explore the detailed animated contrastive AI slideshow</a>: six scenes covering similarity, memory graphs, drift, and governed updates.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 class="heading-element" dir="auto">rUv's work in agentic engineering</h3><a id="user-content-ruvs-work-in-agentic-engineering" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: rUv's work in agentic engineering" href="#ruvs-work-in-agentic-engineering"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/docs/ruvnet-prior-art.md"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/engineering-history.svg" alt="Animated history of rUv's work: 2023 AI tools, 2024 SPARC, 2025 Claude Flow lineage, and 2026 governed systems" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">Reuven Cohen's early agentic engineering work spans <a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/docs/ruvnet-prior-art.md">AI tools and plugins in 2023</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/sparc">SPARC development workflows</a>, and the Claude Flow lineage that became <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/ruflo">Ruflo</a>. That work evolved into coordinated swarms, adaptive memory, portable runtimes, and governed improvement through RuVector, RVF, RVM, and MetaHarness. <a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/docs/ruvnet-prior-art.md">Dated evidence and scoped pioneering claims</a> document specific contributions alongside earlier related work.</p>
<details>
<summary><strong>Connect your agents: federation, skills, command line interface (CLI) and Model Context Protocol (MCP)</strong></summary>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">[!TIP]
<strong>Your team. Your agents. One federation.</strong>
Coordinate at <a href="https://x.ruv.io" rel="nofollow">x.ruv.io</a> · <a href="https://ruflo-federation.ruv.chatgpt.site/" rel="nofollow">RuFlo federation dashboard</a></p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>MCP:</strong> ChatGPT uses <code>https://x.ruv.io/chatgpt/mcp</code>; Claude and service clients use <code>https://x.ruv.io/mcp</code>. Authorize when prompted. <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/ruflo/blob/main/.agents/skills/open-federation/SKILL.md">Setup &amp; details</a>.</p>
</blockquote>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Start with the RuV Stack:</strong> <a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/docs/entrypoint/INSTALL.md">Install skills + marketplace</a> · <a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/plugins/ruvnet/docs/INSTALL.md#local-cli-and-mcp-companion">CLI / MCP guide</a> · <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/metaharness">MetaHarness</a>
Claude Code: <code>/plugin marketplace add ruvnet/ruvnet</code>, then <code>/plugin install ruvnet@ruvnet</code>.
Reviewed changes: <code>node plugins/ruvnet/bin/ruvnet.mjs changes 5 --kind security</code> · snapshot-bound exact evidence with auditable filters and continuation cursors · <a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/docs/self-improvement/2026-09-25.md">Harness evidence</a></p>
</blockquote>
</details>
<p dir="auto">This ecosystem is published through one developer account. Repositories can include community contributions, automation, generated artifacts, and imported history. The evidence files keep those categories separate.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 class="heading-element" dir="auto">Explore the constellation</h2><a id="user-content-explore-the-constellation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Explore the constellation" href="#explore-the-constellation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/assets/ruvnet/constellation.svg"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/constellation.svg" alt="A constellation of capabilities" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>One ecosystem, many ways in.</strong> Start with the problem you want to solve. Follow the constellation from observations to memory, coordinated work, portable execution, and evaluated outcomes.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/assets/ruvnet/constellation-map.svg"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/constellation-map.svg" alt="rUv constellation: perceive with RuView, remember with RuVector, coordinate with Ruflo, execute with RVF and RVM, evaluate with MetaHarness and APx, adapt with Autogenous" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/APx">APx</a> measures agent productivity through Agentic Power (AP), the ratio of accepted reference human work hours to complete elapsed hours; APx is its base 2 logarithm. <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/rvcsi">rvCSI</a> works with Channel State Information (CSI), measurements of how wireless signals propagate through a channel.</p>
<p dir="auto">The connections describe complementary roles. Each project has its own interfaces, maturity, and evidence; this is a conceptual map, not a claim that every system is already integrated.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/assets/ruvnet/capability-cutaway.svg"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/capability-cutaway.svg" alt="Inside the capability loop: illustrated memory layers, coordination routes and evaluation traces" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 class="heading-element" dir="auto">Meet the core systems</h2><a id="user-content-meet-the-core-systems" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Meet the core systems" href="#meet-the-core-systems"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p align="center" dir="auto">
  <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuView" title="Perceive"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/icon-sense.svg" width="56" height="56" alt="Perceive" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 56px;"></a>
  <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuVector" title="Remember"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/icon-memory.svg" width="56" height="56" alt="Remember" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 56px;"></a>
  <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/ruflo" title="Coordinate"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/icon-agents.svg" width="56" height="56" alt="Coordinate" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 56px;"></a>
  <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/rvm" title="Execute"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/icon-runtime.svg" width="56" height="56" alt="Execute" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 56px;"></a>

</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 class="heading-element" dir="auto">What is Cognitum One?</h2><a id="user-content-what-is-cognitum-one" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What is Cognitum One?" href="#what-is-cognitum-one"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://cognitum.one/" rel="nofollow">Cognitum One</a> brings the open source ruvnet stack into enterprise hardware, software, deployment services, and governance systems. ruvnet is the public development ecosystem for experiments, reusable libraries, and community contributions; Cognitum One builds supported, sovereign deployments on that foundation.</p>
<p dir="auto">The platform spans public cloud, customer infrastructure, and the low power edge. Private deployments can operate without external cloud services when the required models, dependencies, and integrations are available locally.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 class="heading-element" dir="auto">How do System 0, System 1, and System 2 fit together?</h3><a id="user-content-how-do-system-0-system-1-and-system-2-fit-together" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How do System 0, System 1, and System 2 fit together?" href="#how-do-system-0-system-1-and-system-2-fit-together"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">In this ecosystem, System 0, System 1, and System 2 describe complementary architectural roles: sensing and response, memory and retrieval, and reasoning and orchestration. These labels are an organizing model, not a universal AI standard or a claim of human cognition.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th align="left">System</th>
<th align="left">Role</th>
<th align="left">Core technologies</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td align="left"><strong>System 0: Sense &amp; Respond</strong></td>
<td align="left">Observe the physical environment and act locally</td>
<td align="left">Cognitum Seed edge hardware, a WebAssembly (WASM) runtime, on device vector storage, and <a href="https://cognitum.one/ruview" rel="nofollow">Cognitum RuView</a> WiFi Channel State Information (CSI) sensing</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td align="left"><strong>System 1: Learn &amp; Remember</strong></td>
<td align="left">Ground work in local data and retain context</td>
<td align="left"><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuVector">RuVector</a> memory and retrieval, with development tools for private data grounding</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td align="left"><strong>System 2: Reason &amp; Orchestrate</strong></td>
<td align="left">Coordinate specialized agents and evaluate their work</td>
<td align="left"><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/ruflo">Ruflo</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/metaharness">MetaHarness</a>, and enterprise deployment services</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://ruos.cognitum.one/" rel="nofollow">ruOS</a> provides the agentic desktop and execution environment connecting these capabilities to applications, files, and terminals.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 class="heading-element" dir="auto">What is ruOS?</h3><a id="user-content-what-is-ruos" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What is ruOS?" href="#what-is-ruos"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Cognitum One <a href="https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20261006476353/en/" rel="nofollow">announced ruOS on October 6, 2026</a>. Teams of agents work inside managed Linux environments on infrastructure the customer controls, with persistent RuVector context, Ruflo coordination, and MetaHarness evaluation. Administrators define access, approval requirements, and action policies.</p>
<p dir="auto">ruOS ships as a standard <code>.deb</code> package and supports local and virtual deployments. Restricted network and air gapped operation requires locally available models and dependencies. <a href="https://ruos.cognitum.one/" rel="nofollow">Explore ruOS</a> · <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/ruos">Public ruOS repository</a> · <a href="https://github.com/cognitum-one">Cognitum repositories</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 class="heading-element" dir="auto">Leadership and advisors</h3><a id="user-content-leadership-and-advisors" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Leadership and advisors" href="#leadership-and-advisors"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://ruv.io/" rel="nofollow">Reuven “rUv” Cohen</a> is Founder and Chief AI Officer; <a href="https://cognitum.one/team" rel="nofollow">D. Christopher Keil</a> is Founding CEO. Founding advisors and investors include Mark B. Templeton, former CEO of Citrix; Dr. Greg Lavender, former CTO of Intel; and John McKinley, former President of AOL Technologies.</p>
<p dir="auto">Strategic advisors include Adrian Cockcroft, Sean Patterson, Tom Lounibos, Jason La Barbera, Stuart Kerr, and Mark Allen, MD, bringing experience across cloud architecture, product, capital, AI workforce adoption, growth, and healthcare. <a href="https://cognitum.one/team" rel="nofollow">Meet the team</a> · <a href="https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260902303667/en/" rel="nofollow">Advisory team announcement</a></p>

<p dir="auto">The dashboard above is generated from this repository's published JSON. Downloads are events, stars cover owned public nonfork repositories, and the project traces connect only two measured snapshots. The dates and counting rules below are part of the numbers.</p>

<p dir="auto">The current npm inventory contains <strong>429 packages</strong>. Official download-range responses were collected for <strong>405 packages</strong>. <strong>3 newly indexed packages</strong> (<code>@agentic-robotics/darwin-arm64</code>, <code>@agentic-robotics/darwin-x64</code>, <code>@agentic-robotics/linux-arm64-gnu</code>) could not be verified (npm download-range endpoint returned HTTP 404 after bounded retries; npm download-range endpoint returned HTTP 404 after bounded retries; npm download-range endpoint returned HTTP 404 after bounded retries) and remain explicitly missing, never zero. <a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/data/registry-stats.json">Registry evidence</a> records both populations. The artifact total combines current npm and crates counts with the 30 historically verified PyPI and Hugging Face artifacts.</p>
<p dir="auto">Package downloads include CI, reinstallations and platform packages. They do not establish unique users. The <a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/.github/workflows/refresh-registry-metrics.yml">weekly refresh</a> updates these measurements from official APIs.</p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Reporting coverage, 2026-11-09 UTC:</strong> These npm figures are observed lower bounds across the 405-package collected cohort (405 of 429 inventory packages). The latest positive cohort day is <strong>2026-11-08</strong>, with <strong>388,290 download events</strong>. This is a completed UTC calendar day, not a rolling 24-hour count. The collected daily responses contain 9 internal all zero cohort days (2026-09-07, 2026-09-08, 2026-09-15, 2026-10-06, 2026-10-14, 2026-10-15, 2026-10-19, 2026-10-20, 2026-10-21); these may be reporting gaps. No trailing all zero days were excluded. 3 inventory packages lacked range evidence and are excluded from totals; no missing events are estimated. <a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/data/snapshots/2026-11-09/npm-downloads.json">Package range evidence</a>.</p>

<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/assets/ruvnet/evolution.svg"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/evolution.svg" alt="An ecosystem in motion" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 class="heading-element" dir="auto">October 2026 update</h2><a id="user-content-october-2026-update" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: October 2026 update" href="#october-2026-update"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">September was about connecting the parts. An agent needs somewhere to work, memory it can carry forward, and a way to show what happened. The work increasingly lives in those connections: Ruflo inside the coding session, RVF between sessions, RuVector behind the memory, and MetaHarness around the decision to keep a change.</p>
<p dir="auto">Ruflo 3.50.0 brings Mods into that loop: prompt routing, tool checks, edit learning, cost controls, and a live swarm pane. The published release keeps Mods opt in and preserves classic hooks as the fallback. Later main commits add default installation and marketplace repair. The release and the moving branch are different evidence points.</p>
<p dir="auto">RVF gives that continuity a concrete form. Memory, metadata, model payloads, and provenance can travel together in a file that a receiver can inspect. RVForge adds authoring, validation, and target staging. The useful boundary is simple: carrying a capability does not grant permission to execute it.</p>
<p dir="auto">The six new public repositories extend the same idea. APx measures accepted work against a human reference. rGi keeps an agent loop durable across restarts. rultra brings governed adaptation to a Pi sensor box. MCP Studio makes the tools accessible through an application. ruPet and Agents of the Dawm explore a more human interface to the work, with the latter still a design proposal.</p>
<p dir="auto">The direction is continuous software with an accountable history. More useful work, less repeated setup, and evidence that survives the session that produced it.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 class="heading-element" dir="auto">New public projects</h3><a id="user-content-new-public-projects" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: New public projects" href="#new-public-projects"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 class="heading-element" dir="auto">RVF: memory that travels with its evidence</h2><a id="user-content-rvf-memory-that-travels-with-its-evidence" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: RVF: memory that travels with its evidence" href="#rvf-memory-that-travels-with-its-evidence"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/assets/ruvnet/memory-journey.svg"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/memory-journey.svg" alt="Portable memory lifecycle: RuVector remembers, RVF packages, RVForge stages, RVM executes" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>RVF is RuVector Format.</strong> It packages segmented vector memory, metadata, model and compute payloads, lineage, and witness records. The aim is to carry useful state from a browser to a local runtime or a governed service without rebuilding the context at every step.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Piece</th>
<th>Responsibility</th>
<th>Evidence</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>RVF format and software development kit (SDK)</td>
<td>Read, write, inspect and query portable memory</td>
<td><a href="https://www.npmjs.com/package/@ruvector/rvf/v/0.3.4" rel="nofollow">SDK 0.3.4</a>, published July 28</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Wire contract</td>
<td>Preserve exact segment and manifest bytes across readers</td>
<td><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuVector/blob/5a93328f2fceb0307c25929ed38cd7a0911fdf00/crates/rvf/rvf-wire/tests/wire_contract_golden.rs">Golden byte tests</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Witness evidence</td>
<td>Bind recorded actions, outcomes, costs and policy results; detect tampering</td>
<td><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuVector/blob/5a93328f2fceb0307c25929ed38cd7a0911fdf00/crates/rvf/rvf-runtime/tests/witness_e2e.rs">Witness integration tests</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>RVForge</td>
<td>Author, validate and stage a canonical RVF for target platforms</td>
<td><a href="https://www.npmjs.com/package/@ruvector/rvforge/v/0.2.0" rel="nofollow">RVForge 0.2.0</a>, published August 5</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Ruflo integration</td>
<td>Portable agent memory and session persistence workflows</td>
<td><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/ruflo/blob/1c78a51d42166cc111e5c24bcf0bb89c51295105/plugins/ruflo-rvf/README.md">RVF plugin contract</a></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">An artifact checksum proves which bytes were examined. A valid signature binds those bytes to a key. Neither proves that the recorded observation is true, that an experiment generalizes, or that execution is authorized. Verification needs a trusted key, an explicit policy, and evidence for the actual workload.</p>
<p dir="auto">RVForge's current local build produces staged bundles. Native installer assembly, platform signing, and runtime validation are separate steps. Backend support varies across RVF native and WASM implementations. Ruflo's session encryption is opt in and does not automatically encrypt exported RVF bytes. These limits are part of the <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuVector/blob/5a93328f2fceb0307c25929ed38cd7a0911fdf00/npm/packages/rvforge/README.md">published contracts</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 class="heading-element" dir="auto">How the stack fits together</h2><a id="user-content-how-the-stack-fits-together" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How the stack fits together" href="#how-the-stack-fits-together"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/metaharness"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/governed-workflow.svg" alt="Animated governed agent workflow: context, coordination and execution, evaluation, revision, and retention under policy" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>RuView, rvCSI, and RuField perceive. Ruflo coordinates. RuVector and AgenticOW remember. RVF carries state and evidence. RVForge stages it. RVM controls execution. MetaHarness evaluates change. Autogenous governs adaptation. APx measures useful work.</strong></p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Layer</th>
<th>Primary systems</th>
<th>Responsibility</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Spatial perception</td>
<td><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuView">RuView</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/rvcsi">rvCSI</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/rufield">RuField</a></td>
<td>Convert RF and multimodal observations into privacy aware spatial evidence</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Sensing governance</td>
<td><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/wifi-veil">WiFi Veil</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuCelium">RuCelium</a></td>
<td>Research sensing boundaries and represent provenance aware environmental observations</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Agent control plane</td>
<td><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/ruflo">Ruflo</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/metaharness">MetaHarness</a></td>
<td>Coordinate agents, route work, evaluate changes, and preserve receipts</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Learning memory</td>
<td><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/RuVector">RuVector</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/agentdb">AgentDB</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/agenticow">AgenticOW</a></td>
<td>Store vector, graph, temporal, episodic, and branchable memory</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Portable execution and transfer</td>
<td><a href="https://www.npmjs.com/package/@ruvector/rvf" rel="nofollow">RVF</a>, <a href="https://www.npmjs.com/package/@ruvector/rvforge" rel="nofollow">RVForge</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/rvQR">rvQR</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/rvm">RVM</a></td>
<td>Package and verify agentic applications, stage target bundles, transfer them optically in alpha, and execute them under capability controls</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Governed adaptation</td>
<td><a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/autogenous">Autogenous</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/dream-machine">Dream Machine</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ruvnet/rGi">rGi</a></td>

</tr>

</tr>
<tr>

<td>Explore world models, visual retrieval, learned optics, and evidence gated decision support</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>

<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/assets/ruvnet/evidence-chain.svg"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/evidence-chain.svg" alt="Evidence chain: collect sources, commit dated JSON snapshots, verify receipts, render SVG and Markdown" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 class="heading-element" dir="auto">The rUv approach to intellectual property</h2><a id="user-content-the-ruv-approach-to-intellectual-property" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: The rUv approach to intellectual property" href="#the-ruv-approach-to-intellectual-property"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/docs/ruvnet-prior-art.md"><img src="/ruvnet/ruvnet/raw/main/assets/ruvnet/open-ip.svg" alt="Animated rUv IP approach: build in public, preserve dated evidence, and deliver commercial value through Cognitum One" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">Build in public. Publish working code, dated evidence, and reproducible results. Credit the foundational research and distinguish original contributions from integration and implementation. The <a href="/ruvnet/ruvnet/blob/main/docs/ruvnet-prior-art.md">prior art dossier</a> records lineage and scoped novelty claims; each repository's license governs reuse.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://cognitum.one/" rel="nofollow">Cognitum One</a> builds commercial value through enterprise integration, hardware, governance, support, and deployment expertise. Public provenance documents contributions; it does not by itself establish patent rights or exclusive ownership.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 class="heading-element" dir="auto">Evidence and provenance</h2><a id="user-content-evidence-and-provenance" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Evidence and provenance" href="#evidence-and-provenance"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto">Repository lineage, public availability, feature evidence, public traction, verified usage, and novelty are distinct claims. A root commit anchors current history. It does not prove that every current feature existed at that date. Download events, clone events, stars, followers, and users are also kept distinct.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 class="heading-element" dir="auto">Project archive</h2><a id="user-content-project-archive" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Project archive" href="#project-archive"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
